Motivation: 完全在线的OPD成本高昂，每次更新需要学生与环境进行新的交互并查询教师，导致训练效率低下。需要一种更高效的方法，既能保持在线策略蒸馏的优势，又能减少环境交互成本。

Method: 提出ReOPD方法：1) 复用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，学生在选定步骤执行动作，教师提供密集的逐步监督；2) 识别并解决"前缀陷阱"——学生on-policy程度提高相关性但可能降低教师目标的可靠性；3) 提出逐步衰减采样策略，优先选择早期、偏移较小的前缀。

Result: 在数学推理和Python搜索环境中，跨多种教师和学生模型规模，ReOPD保持或提升OPD级别的准确率；学生训练期间零工具调用；每训练步骤速度至少比OPD快4倍。

Conclusion: ReOPD将昂贵的智能体-环境交互转化为可复用的离线资源，实现了跨工具、任务和环境的可扩展蒸馏。

Abstract: We study on-policy distillation (OPD) for agentic tasks, where an LLM agent interacts with an environment over multiple turns and a student imitates a teacher over these multi-turn interaction histories. Fully online OPD is costly because each update requires fresh student rollouts through the environment and teacher queries at visited histories. We propose Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation (ReOPD), an off-environment alternative that reuses pre-collected teacher trajectories as replayed prefixes: the student acts at selected steps, while the teacher provides dense per-step supervision without executing new environment interactions. We show that multi-turn OPD introduces a prefix trap: making histories more student-on-policy improves relevance to the student, but can query the teacher on histories where its target is unreliable. This creates a two-sided distribution shift between student occupancy and teacher reliability. ReOPD addresses this by treating multi-turn OPD as a reliability-aware prefix distribution design and implements it with a simple step-decaying sampling schedule that emphasizes early, lower-shift prefixes. Across mathematical reasoning with Python and search environments over multiple teacher and student model scales, ReOPD preserves or improves OPD-level accuracy, uses zero tool calls during student training, and is at least 4$\times$ faster per training step than OPD. ReOPD therefore turns expensive agent-environment interaction into a reusable offline resource, enabling scalable distillation across tools, tasks, and environments.

</details>


### [100] [ACPO: Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization via Fine-Grained Surrogate Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03126)
*Zijun Xie,Yuyang You,Yongzhi Li,Enlei Gong,Zeyu Chen,Quan Chen,Yanhua Cheng,Peng Jiang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACPO框架，通过模态局部替代熵进行令牌级信用分配，在数学推理和编程基准上超越现有RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法在LLM推理中面临稀疏结果奖励导致的令牌级信用分配困难，均匀分配轨迹级奖励或现有熵感知方法存在梯度不对齐问题。

Method: 提出自适应信用策略优化(ACPO)，基于模态局部替代熵进行非对称策略更新：强调成功轨迹中的不确定决策和失败轨迹中的过度自信令牌，理论上保证确定性熵界和策略梯度方向保持。

Result: 在AIME 2025和HumanEvalPro等数学推理和编程基准上，ACPO一致优于DAPO、GTPO和SAPO等强基线。

Conclusion: ACPO通过模态对齐和近端更新有效解决了令牌级信用分配问题，提升了LLM推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), but sparse outcome rewards still make token-level credit assignment difficult. Existing scalable RL methods typically assign trajectory-level rewards uniformly across tokens, while recent entropy-aware approaches either rely on coarse detached heuristics or directly optimize true entropy, which can introduce non-local gradient components misaligned with sampled-token policy updates. We propose Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization (ACPO), a token-level credit assignment framework based on a mode-local surrogate entropy. ACPO asymmetrically modulates policy updates by emphasizing uncertain decisions in successful rollouts and overconfident tokens in failed rollouts. We show that the surrogate admits deterministic entropy bounds and, under modal alignment and proximal updates, preserves the policy-gradient direction to leading order. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, including AIME 2025 and HumanEvalPro, show that ACPO consistently improves over strong RL baselines such as DAPO, GTPO, and SAPO.

</details>


### [101] [Probably Correct Optimal Stable Matching under Two-Sided Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04824)
*Andreas Athanasopoulos,Anne-Marie George,Christos Dimitrakakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双边市场中稳定匹配的序列学习问题，提出了利用"普遍稳定匹配"概念和消除算法，在双方偏好未知且存在噪声反馈的情况下，高效识别最优稳定匹配，并扩展至遗憾最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作大多假设单边偏好已知或仅存在单边不确定性，无法处理现实中双方偏好均未知的情形。本文旨在填补这一空白，设计能够处理双边不确定性和部分偏好信息的算法。

Method: 提出了"普遍稳定匹配"（pervasive stable matching）这一核心概念，用于在部分偏好信息下识别最优稳定匹配；设计了基于消除（elimination-based）的算法，其停止准则利用学习到的部分偏好结构；在半强盗反馈框架下进行样本复杂度分析。

Result: 在纯探索场景下实现了样本复杂度的精细化分析；在遗憾最小化场景下，建立了关于最优稳定匹配的遗憾界，且该界不依赖于最小奖励间隙Δ_min。

Conclusion: 本文在双边不确定性和部分偏好的设置下，为稳定匹配的在线学习提供了理论保证，所提方法在纯探索和遗憾最小化两个任务上均优于现有结果。

Abstract: We study a sequential learning problem for stable matchings in two-sided markets where preferences on both sides are initially unknown. We focus on a centralized setting where an algorithm matches agents at each time step and receives noisy rewards that reflect the preferences of the matched agents, following a semi-bandit feedback structure. We adopt a pure exploration perspective, aiming to efficiently identify the optimal stable matching with high probability. Our work extends prior results by handling \emph{two-sided uncertainty} and by exploiting \emph{partial preference} information. A central ingredient is the notion of \textbf{pervasive stable matching}, which enables the identification of optimal stable matchings under partial preferences. We propose elimination-based algorithms whose stopping criteria exploit the structure of the learned partial preferences, and provide a refined sample-complexity analysis. Beyond pure exploration, we extend our approach to regret minimization and establish regret bounds with respect to the \emph{optimal} stable matching that avoid dependence on the minimum reward gap $Δ_{\min}$.

</details>


### [102] [Anticipatory Reinforcement Learning for Trajectory Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03132)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对工业控制中深度强化学习因纯反应式控制导致的滞后和超调问题，本文提出一种预测性DRL方法，通过将目标速度与未来参考轨迹纳入状态空间实现前瞻控制。在1-DoF直升机平台上，仿真将平均绝对偏差从2.73°降至0.31°（误差降低9倍），但零样本迁移到实物时存在sim-to-real差距。有趣的是，仅使用单一步长预测的简化配置在实物上达到了与最复杂模型相同的最高性能（1.11°），表明高粒度预测数据并非物理迁移的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制中DRL因仅依赖当前跟踪误差进行纯反应式控制，导致滞后和超调问题，而现有前瞻控制方法计算开销过大。

Method: 提出预测性DRL框架，在DRL状态空间中增加目标速度与未来参考轨迹信息；使用PPO算法在1-DoF直升机平台上评估8种不同配置。

Result: 仿真中最佳配置将平均绝对偏差从2.73°降至0.31°（误差减少9倍）；实物测试中，使用单一步长前瞻的简化配置与最复杂模型性能相当（1.11°）。

Conclusion: 预测性状态增强能有效提升DRL控制性能，但复杂的多步预测信息在物理迁移时并不必要，简洁的预测配置即可达到最优实物性能。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) in industrial control often suffers from lag and overshoot due to purely reactive control based on the current tracking error. To achieve anticipatory control without high computational overhead, we introduce a predictive formulation that augments the DRL state space with target velocities and future reference horizons. Evaluating eight configurations using proximal policy optimization (PPO) on a 1-degree-of-freedom (1-DoF) helicopter testbed, simulation results showed a 9-fold error reduction, lowering the mean absolute deviation from 2.73° to 0.31°. However, zero-shot transfer to physical hardware revealed a sim-to-real gap. Interestingly, a simpler configuration using a single, further look-ahead horizon matched the real-world top performance of the most complex model (1.11°). Overall, evaluating various combinations of prediction horizons and target velocities demonstrated that highly granular predictive data is not necessarily required for physical transfer.

</details>


### [103] [Active Learning on Adversarially Corrupted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04869)
*Marco Bressan,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Tommaso d`Orsi,Emmanuel Esposito,Silvio Lattanzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性环境下通过主动学习高效识别图中被恶意篡改的顶点子集的问题，提出一种查询复杂度与攻击者能力和图的顶点扩展性呈多项式关系的近似恢复算法，并首次揭示了顶点扩展性在对抗性主动学习查询复杂度中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中存在恶意实体篡改网络结构（如添加边）以隐藏被破坏的顶点，现有方法缺乏对此类结构性对抗攻击的鲁棒性，亟需设计能够高效识别被篡改顶点的主动学习算法。

Method: 定义了对抗者通过添加边（包括被篡改顶点之间、被篡改顶点与原始图之间）来隐藏“被破坏顶点集”的威胁模型，以被破坏顶点在原始图中的邻域大小衡量攻击者能力；采用主动学习框架，将问题转化为带基数约束的最小顶点扩展集搜索问题；通过适配平方和（Sum-of-Squares）方法设计多项式时间算法来近似求解该组合优化问题。

Result: 提出了一个高效的近似恢复算法，其查询复杂度与攻击者能力（邻域大小）和原始图的顶点扩展性成多项式关系；首次证明了顶点扩展性是决定对抗性主动学习查询复杂度的关键图论度量。

Conclusion: 顶点扩展性在对抗性主动学习的查询复杂度中起关键作用；所提出的平方和算法为带基数约束的最小顶点扩展问题提供了多项式时间近似解法，为后续鲁棒主动学习研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Motivated by real-world scenarios where malicious entities tamper with existing networks, we define a model where an adversary seeks to hide a set of \emph{corrupted vertices} inside a graph $G^*$. To this end, the adversary can add edges between the corrupted vertices, as well as edges between the corrupted vertices and $G^*$, and its power is then measured by the size of the \emph{neighborhood} of the corrupted vertices in $G^*$. Our goal is to design an active learning algorithm that efficiently finds the subset of corrupted vertices using a small number of label queries. We devise an efficient algorithm that approximately recovers the corrupted vertices with a query complexity that depends polynomially on both the power of the adversary and the \emph{vertex expansion} of $G^*$, a fundamental measure of graph connectivity. At the heart of this result is a polynomial-time algorithm, obtained by carefully adapting sum-of-squares algorithms for approximating minimum expansion, that finds a set with small vertex expansion subject to cardinality constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that the vertex expansion is shown to play a key role in determining the query complexity of active learning algorithms robust to structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [104] [Sample-Efficient Pareto Front Modeling for Energy-Aware Reinforcement Learning Using Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03140)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）的自动化权重选择方法，用于在强化学习框架下高效发现Pareto最优折衷策略，在Quanser Aero 2俯仰控制实验平台上，相比均匀网格搜索，MOBO以更少的评估预算获得了更优的超体积和最大散布指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化中需要在运行性能与能效之间权衡，传统方法依赖人工设定线性组合的奖励函数权重，耗时且易引入偏差，难以发现最优折衷解。

Method: 将权重选择过程建模为多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）问题，采用期望超体积改进（qEHVI）采集函数进行高效采样，在1-DoF俯仰控制的Quanser Aero 2平台上验证。

Result: MOBO方法在超体积和最大散布两个指标上均优于均匀网格搜索，能以更少的评估预算找到高质量、多样化的折衷策略。

Conclusion: MOBO能够自动化、高效地发现Pareto前沿，为复杂机电系统的能量感知控制提供了一种样本高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial automation increasingly demands control strategies that balance operational performance with strict energy efficiency requirements. A common approach to solving this multi-objective problem, particularly within the framework of reinforcement learning (RL), is to formulate a single, scalar reward function that linearly combines the competing objectives. However, the manual weighting of these different objectives is heavily reliant on domain intuition, incredibly time-consuming, prone to human bias, and frequently fails to uncover optimal trade-off solutions. This work addresses the critical challenge of automating the weight selection process to systematically and efficiently discover the Pareto front of optimal trade-off policies. We formulate the weight selection process as a multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) problem and evaluate its sample efficiency against a standard uniform grid search baseline. Using a physical Quanser Aero 2 testbed configured for 1-DoF pitch control, our results demonstrate that the MOBO approach, utilizing the expected hypervolume improvement (qEHVI) acquisition function, consistently outperforms uniform grid sampling. MOBO achieves superior hypervolume and maximum spread, successfully identifying high-quality, diverse trade-off policies with a reduced evaluation budget, thereby enabling highly efficient energy-aware control in complex mechatronic systems.

</details>


### [105] [Regime-Conditional Stabilisation of LLM-Augmented Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04470)
*Faid Keddouri,Sohaib Houhou,Aissa Boulmerka,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）中动态更新LLM生成的奖励权重会违反势能基奖励塑形（PBRS）的平稳性假设，污染经验回放缓冲区，导致性能崩溃。作者提出两种稳定策略（基于阶段的冻结调度和指数移动平均平滑），并在三个环境中验证了三种机制：增补性（基线可用时，EMA提升12.3%）、必需性（基线无效时，任何塑形都能解锁任务）、补充性（基线接近饱和时，稳定塑形保持性能）。结论是奖励信号平稳性是必要设计约束，机制位置可预测动态LLM塑形的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为MARL提供了一种将人类目标转化为奖励信号的自然接口，但训练过程中动态更新LLM生成的奖励权重对MARL的影响尚不明确。本文旨在揭示这一动态集成过程中存在的平稳性违反问题及其后果，并提出稳定策略。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明动态更新LLM生成的奖励权重会违反PBRS的平稳性假设，导致经验回放缓冲区的奖励标签过时。2. 提出两种稳定策略：基于阶段的冻结调度（Phase-Based Freeze Schedule）和指数移动平均平滑（EMA）。3. 在三个合作环境（Simple Spread、Level-Based Foraging、SMAC 3m）中使用QMIX算法进行实验，并辅以VDN扩展。

Result: 三机制分类结果：增补性机制（Simple Spread，基线74.4%）：EMA提升至86.7%（+12.3%），动态更新崩溃至15.2%；必需性机制（Level-Based Foraging，基线0.1%）：任何塑形解锁任务至95.9%（EMA下）；补充性机制（SMAC 3m，基线98.8%）：稳定塑形保持99.9%，非稳定塑形增加方差无增益。

Conclusion: 奖励信号平稳性是LLM塑形MARL中的必要设计约束；机制位置（基线能力水平）是预测动态LLM塑形效果的实际指标。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a natural interface for translating human objectives into reward signals for cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), yet the training-time dynamics of this integration remain poorly understood. We show that dynamically updating LLM-generated reward weights during off-policy MARL violates the stationarity assumption of Potential-Based Reward Shaping (PBRS) and contaminates the experience replay buffer, whose stored transitions carry reward labels computed under stale shaping weights. We characterise the result as a regime-dependent failure whose severity depends on how competent the unshaped baseline already is. To control it we propose two stabilisation strategies: a Phase-Based Freeze Schedule that enforces strict stationarity within training phases, and Exponential Moving Average (EMA) smoothing that bounds per-episode weight drift. We evaluate across three cooperative environments and five random seeds with QMIX, complemented by an exploratory VDN extension, yielding a three-regime taxonomy. In the augmentative regime (Simple Spread), where the baseline is functional (74.4 %), EMA significantly improves success to 86.7 % ($+12.3$ pp, $p<0.01$) while naive dynamic updates collapse it to 15.2 %. In the essential regime (Level-Based Foraging), where the baseline is broken (0.1 %), any shaping unlocks the task (95.9 % under EMA). In the supplementary regime (SMAC 3m), where the baseline is near-saturated (98.8 %), stabilised shaping preserves performance (99.9 %) while unstabilised shaping adds variance without gain. These findings establish reward-signal stationarity as a necessary design constraint and indicate that regime placement is a practical predictor of whether dynamic LLM shaping helps or harms.

</details>


### [106] [Geometry-Aware Bayesian Quantification via Compositional Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04977)
*Alejandro Moreo,Pablo González,Juan José del Coz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Aitchison几何和对数比表示的KDE模型用于多类量化（标签偏移下的类别先验估计），通过考虑单纯形几何和收缩正则化，在42个数据集上取得了与最先进方法竞争的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于KDE的量化方法使用欧几里得高斯核，忽略了后验向量位于概率单纯形上的几何结构，导致概率质量被错误地分配到单纯形边界之外，影响估计精度。

Method: 1) 基于对数比表示和Aitchison几何构建几何感知的KDE模型；2) 引入收缩正则化以提高在单纯形边界附近的鲁棒性；3) 结合KDE量化的最大似然解释，推导了点估计和贝叶斯推断两种类别先验估计方法。

Result: 在42个数据集（涵盖表格、文本和图像领域）上的实验表明，该方法与最先进的量化器具有竞争力，通常优于标准KDE基线，同时在贝叶斯量化方法中也表现强劲。

Conclusion: 考虑后验向量的单纯形几何结构（Aitchison几何）能够有效提升KDE-based量化方法的性能，特别是结合收缩正则化后，在边界附近的鲁棒性得到增强。

Abstract: Accurately estimating the unknown target label distribution is the critical first step for adapting to label shift. This task, widely known as quantification or class prevalence estimation, has recently seen significant advances through continuous KDE-based methods which model the density of multiclass classifier posteriors. Posterior vectors might be regarded as compositional data, since they lie on the probability simplex. However, existing KDE-based quantifiers typically rely on Euclidean Gaussian kernels, which ignore simplex geometry and incorrectly assign probability mass outside its boundaries. We introduce a geometry-aware KDE model for multiclass quantification based on log-ratio representations and Aitchison geometry, together with a shrinkage regularization that improves robustness near the simplex boundary. Combined with a maximum-likelihood interpretation of KDE-based quantification, we derive both point-estimation and Bayesian inference procedures for class prevalences. Experiments on 42 datasets across tabular, text, and image domains show that the proposed method is competitive with state-of-the-art quantifiers, often improving over standard KDE-based baselines, while also yielding strong results among Bayesian quantification methods.

</details>


### [107] [Two Black Boxes, One Solver: Encoder Probing and Decoder Attribution for Neural Multi-Attribute VRP under Hard-Mask and Recourse Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04487)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多属性车辆路径问题（MAVRP）的神经自回归求解器，提出了一种同时打开编码器和解码器两个黑箱的解释性协议。编码器侧通过线性探针、自组织度量、秩基丰富度分析和发现方向分析来表征潜在表示中的约束信息；解码器侧使用三种归因方法（梯度、积分梯度、DeepLIFT）提供溯因、对比和反事实三种解释角度。实验发现：图归纳偏置提升表示可预测性和解码器合理性；MoE编码器以分布式方式表示约束；Recourse训练策略能产生有用的可修复反事实，而Hard-Mask策略无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经自回归求解器在MAVRP上虽能达到有竞争力的成本，但缺乏每步决策的可解释性，导致调度员无法验证、接受或比较这些方案，亟需打开编码器和解码器的黑箱。

Method: 提出统一协议：编码器侧采用线性探针、自组织度量（自发组织指标）、秩基丰富度分析及发现方向分析（含干预验证）来揭示潜在表示如何表征图、节点、边层面的约束族；解码器侧使用三种归因方法（梯度、积分梯度、DeepLIFT）提供溯因、对比（与最佳可行替代方案对比）和反事实（改变决策或恢复可行性的最小输入变化）三种解释视角，并从保真度、集中度、稳定性、合理性和可行动性五个维度评分。

Result: 在三种编码器（Attention baseline、Unimp、UnimpMoe）与两种解码器（Hard-Mask、Recourse）组合的六个变体上实验发现：图归纳偏置能同时提升编码器表示的线型可预测性和解码器解释的合理性；MoE编码器以分布式而非轴对齐方式表征约束；Recourse训练策略（而非其较软掩码本身）产生的策略能有效表示不可行性，生成有意义的可修复反事实，而Hard-Mask策略即使外部注入不可行备选方案也无法产生。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双黑箱解释协议能有效揭示MAVRP神经求解器的内部表征和决策逻辑，并表明图归纳偏置和Recourse训练策略对提升可解释性具有重要价值。

Abstract: Neural autoregressive solvers for the Multi-Attribute Vehicle Routing Problem (MAVRP) reach competitive cost but offer no per-step justification, a problem when dispatchers must validate, accept, or compare them. We open two complementary black boxes in one protocol. On the encoder side, linear probes, spontaneous-organization metrics, rank-based richness measures, and discovered-direction analyses with intervention validation characterize how the latent represents constraint families at the graph, node, and edge level. On the decoder side, three attribution methods (gradient, integrated gradients, DeepLIFT) feed three reading angles: abductive, contrastive against the best feasible alternative, and counterfactual (smallest input change that switches the action or restores feasibility). Explanations are scored on fidelity, concentration, stability, sanity, and actionability. Across six variants combining three encoders (Attention baseline, Unimp, UnimpMoe) with two decoders (Hard-Mask, Recourse), we find that graph inductive bias improves both representational predictability and decoder sanity, that the Mixture-of-Experts encoder represents constraints in a distributed rather than axis-aligned way, and that the Recourse training regime, not merely its softer mask, produces policies that represent infeasibility usefully, exposing make-feasible counterfactuals that Hard-Mask policies fail to produce even when fed infeasible alternatives externally.

</details>


### [108] [Functional Bilevel Optimization for Predictive Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05098)
*Ieva Petrulionyte,Julien Mairal,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过DPVar（条件均值预测的方差）实现平均人口统计均等，并设计了两种算法（FBO和ITD）来优化这一功能双层问题，在合成数据和60个表格回归数据集上取得了优于现有方法的公平-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当敏感属性为连续且高维时（如人口统计学得分向量、后验分布、年龄或收入分布），强制完全统计独立性往往过于严格，而现有松弛方法依赖间接依赖惩罚或对抗性方案，未能直接优化公平-精度权衡。

Method: 1) 提出DPVar（给定敏感属性条件下条件均值预测的方差）作为平均人口统计均等的度量；2) 将优化问题建模为功能双层问题；3) 提出FBO算法（利用平方损失下的闭形式伴随矩阵计算精确超梯度）和ITD算法（通过展开内层步骤进行微分，可推广到平方损失以外的情形）。

Result: 在合成数据和包含60个表格回归数据集的半合成基准上，两种方法均实现了最低或接近最低的聚合公平-精度遗憾，并持续匹配或优于HSIC、对抗性、线性依赖和广义DP等强基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过直接优化公平-精度权衡，在连续高维敏感属性场景下实现了更优的公平性约束放松方案。

Abstract: When sensitive attributes are continuous and high-dimensional $-$ demographic score vectors, posteriors over attributes, age or income profiles $-$ enforcing full statistical independence is often too restrictive, and existing relaxations rely on indirect dependence penalties or adversarial schemes that do not directly target the fairness-accuracy trade-off. We instead consider mean demographic parity through DPVar, the variance of the conditional-mean prediction given the sensitive attribute, and show that optimizing it yields a functional bilevel problem. We propose two algorithms for this problem: FBO, which uses a closed-form adjoint we derive for the squared-loss case to obtain an exact hypergradient, and ITD, which differentiates through unrolled inner steps and extends beyond squared loss. On synthetic data and a new semi-synthetic benchmark built from 60 tabular regression datasets, both methods achieve the lowest or near-lowest aggregate fairness-accuracy regret, and consistently match or outperform strong HSIC, adversarial, linear-dependence, and generalized-DP baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Rethinking Neural Nonlinearity as Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03148)
*Muhammad Sabih,Frank Hannig,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出阈值门控（Threshold Gating, TG）作为一种通用原始算子，证明标准激活函数（ReLU、SiLU、Sigmoid等）均为TG的特例，并验证其可在不重新训练的情况下替换预训练网络中的激活函数，同时支持从头训练，在模型压缩、性能和训练速度方面带来提升。此外，提出“最小分支定理”，并指出TG在模拟存内计算系统中可统一实现，解决ADC/DAC瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明非线性不仅可通过激活函数实现，还可通过输入条件化的分支阈值门控作为通用原始算子实现，并揭示标准激活函数本质上都是该原始算子的特例，从而为硬件统一实现和模型压缩提供理论基础。

Method: 提出阈值门控（Threshold Gating）原始算子，将分段线性（ReLU、PReLU、Hardtanh）和平滑（SiLU、Sigmoid、Tanh、GELU）激活函数统一为该原语实例；对Softmax通过逐元素Sigmoid等价形式进行精确TG转换；设计“最小分支定理”关联分支数量与深度神经网络可训练性。

Result: 转换预训练网络（CNN、Transformer、RNN）后模型性能保持不变且无需重新训练；从头训练时TG能超越简单替换激活函数，在模型压缩、性能和训练时间上取得增益；模拟存内计算系统中TG可统一实现，缓解ADC/DAC瓶颈。

Conclusion: 阈值门控是一种统一的、通用的非线性原始算子，能够等价表示主流激活函数，兼具理论完备性与硬件友好性，对神经网络设计与硬件加速具有重要价值。

Abstract: Activation functions are considered an essential primitive for neural nonlinearity, i.e., they enable neural networks to serve as universal approximators. In this paper, we show that this nonlinearity can also be achieved by input-conditioned threshold gating through branches as a universal primitive. We demonstrate that standard activations -- whether piecewise-linear (ReLU, PReLU, Hardtanh) or smooth (SiLU, Sigmoid, Tanh, GELU) -- are in fact instances of a single Threshold Gating (TG) primitive. For softmax, we show that it admits an exact TG conversion via its equivalent per-element Sigmoid form. We then validate these equivalences by converting pretrained networks across CNNs, transformer-based models, and recurrent architectures, preserving model performance without requiring retraining. Threshold Gating also enables training from scratch that goes beyond replacing existing activations, enabling gains in model compression, performance, and shorter training. We also propose a 'Minimal Branch Theorem' which relates the minimum number of required branches in our primitive to the trainability of general deep neural networks. In terms of hardware implementation, TG maps to a unified implementation in the case of analog in-memory systems, addressing the bottleneck of analog-to-digital and digital-to-analog converters (ADC/DAC) that is known to significantly impact power consumption and on-chip area.

</details>


### [110] [TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05339)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Kayhan Behdin,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Wenhui Zhu,Xinchen Du,Aida Rahmattalabi,Ran He,Sen Na,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREK是一种两阶段训练方法，先用正向KL散度将教师模型（外部或自增强）的高质量推理路径拉入学生策略支持域，再切换回标准GRPO进行在线强化学习。相比纯GRPO，TREK在数学推理（AIME）和智能体任务（ALFWorld、ScienceWorld）上显著提升性能，尤其是在困难样本上加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在策略当前采样的推理轨迹有用时效果良好，但在面对正确答案模式位于学生on-policy支持域之外的高难度提示词时，会陷入停滞。为此，需要一种能扩展学生支持域的方法，让模型能够探索并学习原本无法产生的正确推理路径。

Method: TREK分步执行：(1) 识别学生无辅助时通过率极低的提示词；(2) 向提案源（外部黑盒教师、白盒教师或带额外推理上下文的同一模型）查询并生成验证过的候选解；(3) 按当前学生似然保留top-r个候选；(4) 进行短周期的正向KL散度训练，将这些验证过的解模式拉入学生支持域；(5) 返回标准on-policy GRPO微调阶段。

Result: 在数学推理上，TREK使用DeepSeek-V4作为教师，在AIME 2025上将Qwen3-8B从36.9提升至40.3，在AIME 2024上从47.9提升至51.1（avg@16）；自上下文变体无需外部教师即可达到38.5和49.6。在智能体任务上，ALFWorld成功率从75.8提升到82.8，ScienceWorld成功率从12.5提升到26.7，且最难任务类型上训练初期即达到高成功率。

Conclusion: TREK通过蒸馏（正向KL）扩展策略支持域而非模仿教师行为，是一种通用且高效的预训练阶段辅助方法，能有效解决GRPO在困难样本上的停滞问题，并兼容多种教师形式。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is effective when the current policy already samples useful reasoning trajectories, but it stalls on hard prompts whose correct solution modes lie outside the student's on-policy support. We propose TREK (Teacher-Routed Exploration via Forward KL), a simple staged procedure that uses distillation not for imitation but for exploration support expansion. A key advantage of TREK is its generality: because it only consumes verified output trajectories, it can use an external black-box teacher, a white-box teacher, or the same model given additional inference-time context, and it can efficiently identify which hard-prompt samples are most worth consolidating even when teacher internals are unavailable. TREK first identifies prompts where the unaided student has very low pass rate, queries a proposal source to produce verified candidate solutions, keeps the top-$r$ proposals ranked by current student likelihood, applies a short forward-KL phase to pull those verified modes into the student's support, and then returns to standard on-policy GRPO refinement. On mathematical reasoning, TREK with DeepSeek-V4 proposals improves Qwen3 models across all tested scales on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025; for Qwen3-8B, it improves AIME 2025 from 36.9 to 40.3 and AIME 2024 from 47.9 to 51.1 (avg@16), while the self-context variant reaches 38.5 and 49.6 without an external teacher. On agentic tasks, TREK raises ALFWorld success rate from 75.8 to 82.8 and ScienceWorld success rate from 12.5 to 26.7; notably, on the hardest task types, TREK achieves high success rates early in training while unaided GRPO requires substantially more optimization steps to reach comparable levels.

</details>


### [111] [Decentralised Federated Learning over Temporal Networks: The Role of Heterogeneities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03171)
*Arash Badie-Modiri,Chiara Boldrini,Lorenzo Valerio,János Kertész,Márton Karsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了去中心化联邦学习在时序网络上的训练过程本质上是懒随机游走扩散过程，并证明忽略通信网络的时空异质性会导致不切实际的快速收敛假象，真实网络中异质性会显著减慢收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习中结构性和时序性异质性的影响尚未被充分理解，现有研究往往忽略了这些因素对训练过程的真实影响。

Method: 通过将局部平均聚合模型参数的训练过程映射为时序网络上的懒随机游走扩散过程，分析了早期阶段和稳态极限下的动力学行为，并在真实世界时序网络上进行验证。

Result: 发现去中心化联邦学习的收敛过程与时序网络上的懒随机游走扩散动力学一致；典型的实验设置由于忽略时空异质性而得出过于乐观的快速收敛结果；真实网络中的异质性通常大幅减慢扩散和收敛速度。

Conclusion: 去中心化联邦学习的实际收敛速度受到通信网络结构和时序特性的显著制约，在评估和设计系统时必须考虑这些真实世界的异质性因素。

Abstract: Decentralised federated learning, based on peer-to-peer communication, is increasingly proposed for on-device training of machine learning models, promising a privacy-preserving, communication-efficient training process with no risk of single-point failure. However, the role of structural and temporal inhomogeneities in such fully decentralised settings remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate their effects when model parameters are locally averaged during aggregation. We show that the decentralised federated learning process is governed, both in the early phase and the late, stationary limit, by the same dynamics as a lazy random-walk diffusion process on temporal networks. Based on this mapping, we demonstrate that the typical experimental scenario used in decentralised federated learning leads to unrealistically rapid convergence because of ignoring the temporal and structural inhomogeneities inherent in the communication network. We analyse real-world temporal networks and find that inhomogeneities most often dramatically slow down diffusion, hence the convergence process.

</details>


### [112] [What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05381)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格推导了连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的证据下界（ELBO），证明了Oracle距离定理：负ELBO恰好等于数据熵加上从Oracle逆过程到学习过程的路径KL散度，而非仅仅是上界。其唯一优化器是真实逆跳转率在当前噪声状态下的条件期望，不可约代价是前向过程Z_t破坏干净数据Z_0信息的速率-d/dt I(Z_0; Z_t)。对于具有token分解噪声的序列，优化器有三种精确坐标形式：去噪器、cavity（桥接插件）和得分，且它们可相互转换。该框架统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD和GIDD等现有方法，解释了去噪器和cavity在掩码扩散中一致而在均匀扩散中不同的原因，证明了去噪器参数化会导致均匀ELBO在初始化时发散而桥接插件保持有限，并在初始化时精确校准ELBO实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在跳转率层面，去噪器、得分比和桥接插件预测器本质上是同一对象在不同坐标下的表示，用错误坐标读取神经网络会改变训练和采样的过程。现有文献中不同损失函数实际优化的是哪个分布律尚不明确，需要建立统一的理论框架来澄清这些关系。

Method: 从连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）ELBO的严格推导出发，包含边界项，证明了Oracle距离定理。对于具有token分解噪声的序列，推导了优化器的三种精确坐标形式（去噪器、cavity、得分）及其闭式转换关系。

Result: 负ELBO等于数据熵加路径KL散度；优化器是真实逆跳转率的条件期望；不可约代价是前向过程破坏信息的速率-d/dt I(Z_0; Z_t)；所有噪声过程共享相同的最佳负ELBO（数据熵）；统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD等现有方法；去噪器和cavity在掩码扩散中一致但在均匀扩散中不同；均匀扩散中去噪器参数化导致ELBO初始化发散而桥接插件保持有限。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的不同参数化形式（去噪器、得分、桥接插件）本质上是同一优化器在不同坐标下的表示，选择错误的参数化会导致训练和采样过程偏离目标。该理论框架为理解和设计离散扩散模型提供了统一的视角和精确的数值验证。

Abstract: What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? At the level of jump rates, these are one object in different coordinates, and reading a neural network in the wrong coordinate changes the process being trained and sampled. Starting with a rigorous derivation of the continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) ELBO for any noising process, boundary terms included, we prove the \emph{Oracle Distance} theorem: the negative ELBO is exactly equal to the data entropy plus the path KL from the oracle reverse process to the learned one, not merely a bound. Its unique optimizer is therefore the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate given the current noisy state, and its irreducible cost is the rate at which the forward process $Z_t$ destroys information about the clean data $Z_0$, $-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$, so every noising process shares the same best achievable negative ELBO: the data entropy. For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. This framework identifies which law each loss in the literature actually optimizes, recovering MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD as special cases; explains why denoiser and cavity coincide for masked diffusion but not for uniform diffusion; proves that a denoiser parameterization makes the uniform ELBO diverge at initialization while the bridge plug-in stays finite; and calibrates ELBO implementations exactly at initialization. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.

</details>


### [113] [Understanding electricity consumption behaviour through Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03176)
*Enrico Cofler,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Matteo Giuliani,Andrea Castelletti,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用逆强化学习将家庭视为智能体，从意大利电力消费集群中提取隐含的奖励函数，揭示不同消费者群体在2021-2023年能源危机和热浪冲击下制冷行为的异质性响应，发现消费行为的调整呈现短暂、持久或微弱的差异化模式，并指出消费时段是独立于社会经济环境的行为异质性维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度简化了家庭电力消费对热应激的非线性响应，未能捕捉不同收入、消费习惯和建筑环境下消费者的异质性行为，尤其在气候变暖和地缘政治紧张的背景下，决策者需要更精细的理解来制定有效的能源政策。

Method: 将家庭视为与复杂环境交互的智能体，采用逆强化学习（IRL）从观测到的消费行为中推断模型隐含的奖励函数；对意大利电力消费集群进行聚类，分别提取各集群的奖励函数；对比2021年夏季（能源危机和热浪前）、2022年（期间）和2023年（后）的制冷行为变化。

Result: 冲击对不同消费者群体呈现异质性重塑：部分群体为短暂调整（冲击缓解后恢复），部分为持久性转变（持续至2023年），部分几乎无变化；在日内时间尺度上，社会经济和环境背景相似的群体因消费时段不同而表现出截然不同的响应，表明消费时段是独立的行为异质性维度。

Conclusion: 能源政策和需求响应方案不仅应考虑消费者的身份和居住地，还应考虑其消费时段，以及其对冲击的响应是否具有持续性。

Abstract: Understanding how households consume electricity in response to socioeconomic and climatic drivers is important for decision-makers designing energy policies in a changing climate and under geopolitical tensions. Consumers respond differently to thermal stress depending on income, consumption habits and the surrounding built environment, a nonlinear behaviour that most approaches oversimplify. In this study, households are treated as agents interacting with complex environments, and Inverse Reinforcement Learning is used to represent their consumption behaviour as model implied reward functions. Specifically, we observe how these reward functions change when households undergo socioeconomic and climatic shocks. The framework is tested on different clusters of electricity consumption profiles in Italy. Clusters' reward functions are retrieved and used to understand how cooling behaviour changes from summer 2021 to summer 2022 and 2023, before, during and after the energy crisis and a heatwave. We find that these shocks reshaped cooling behaviour heterogeneously across consumer groups, in directions conditioned by their prior habits and built environment. Across the 2021 to 2023 summers, we identify a spectrum of responses: transient adjustments that receded as the shocks eased, durable shifts persisting into 2023, and consumers exhibiting negligible change. At the intradaily scale, groups comparable in socioeconomic and environmental context but differing in their daily timing of consumption responded distinctly, identifying time of use as a separate dimension of behavioural heterogeneity. Energy policies and demand-response schemes should therefore account not only for who consumers are and where they live, but for when they consume and whether their response to a shock persists.

</details>


### [114] [A Bayesian Framework for Evaluating Scenario Compatibility in Generative Population Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03190)
*Zhenlin Qin,Leizhen Wang,Yancheng Ling,Zhenliang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集成贝叶斯更新的框架，用于量化情景级约束与生成式人口合成模型之间的兼容性。通过开发人口感知条件变分自编码器，将情景目标作为概率证据，利用有效样本量（ESS）评估情景对结构不确定性的压缩程度，揭示目标与模型学习到的联合结构之间的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于情景的交通分析通过聚合人口目标设定未来假设，而生成式人口合成模型产生细粒度的个体级实现。当情景目标施加于生成模型时，现行方法依赖确定性边际校准，隐含假设目标与模型学习到的结构支持兼容。然而，情景级约束是否位于生成支持内以及它们对结构不确定性的扭曲程度尚未得到充分检验。

Method: 提出集成贝叶斯更新框架：(1) 开发人口感知条件变分自编码器，学习保留聚合保真度的合理人口结构分布；(2) 从学习到的先验中采样集成实现，提供结构不确定性的经验近似；(3) 将情景目标作为聚合统计量的概率证据，通过贝叶斯更新在集成上获得后验权重；(4) 使用有效样本量（ESS）量化情景兼容性，衡量后验集中度和条件诱导的结构不确定性压缩程度。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 情景影响不仅取决于目标幅度，还取决于与学习到的联合结构的对齐程度；(2) 当目标落在先验集成支持范围之外时，会暴露出结构性失效模式；(3) ESS能够有效量化情景对结构不确定性的压缩效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为在下游投影和交通规划之前评估情景可行性和结构一致性提供了一种概率诊断工具，克服了传统确定性边际校准无法检测情景与模型结构之间兼容性的局限。

Abstract: Scenario-based transportation analysis specifies future assumptions through aggregate population targets, whereas generative population synthesis models produce detailed individual-level realizations. When scenario targets are imposed on generative models, current practice relies on deterministic marginal calibration, implicitly assuming that the targets are compatible with the model's learned structural support. However, whether scenario-level constraints lie within the generative support--and how strongly they distort structural uncertainty--remains largely unexamined. We propose an ensemble-based Bayesian updating framework to quantify scenario compatibility in conditional population synthesis. A population-aware conditional variational autoencoder is developed to learn a distribution over plausible population structures while preserving aggregate fidelity. An ensemble of realizations sampled from the learned prior provides an empirical approximation of structural uncertainty. Scenario targets are treated as probabilistic evidence over aggregate statistics, and posterior weights are obtained through Bayesian updating across the ensemble. Scenario compatibility is quantified using effective sample size (ESS), which measures posterior concentration and the compression of structural uncertainty induced by conditioning. Experiments demonstrate that scenario impact depends not only on target magnitude but also on alignment with the learned joint structure, and reveal structural failure modes when targets fall outside prior ensemble support. The proposed framework provides a probabilistic diagnostic model for evaluating scenario feasibility and structural consistency before downstream projection and transportation planning.

</details>


### [115] [Reduced-Order Models: The Mother of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03198)
*Rajat Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文追溯了世界模型的功能解剖学在三个学术共同体（模型降阶、特征脸方法、基于测量的POD框架）中的独立发展，指出其核心组件（编码-解码器、潜在动力学、动作条件预测）早在现代自监督学习之前就已存在。模型降阶贡献了可验证性、物理基础和极端数据效率，而学习型世界模型贡献了非线性表示、可迁移性和长时域。作者认为将世界模型部署于安全关键系统的最大障碍不是预测精度而是可验证性，并提出了融合两条研究路线的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示现代世界模型的核心组件在模型降阶与控制理论中已被独立发明、部署和形式化分析，从而弥合两个社区之间的知识鸿沟，为安全关键系统中的世界模型部署铺平道路。

Method: 跨学科历史追溯：分别从模型降阶（基于POD的湍流模型）、早期计算机视觉（特征脸方法中的编码-解码器和运行时有效性检查）、以及基于测量的POD框架（设施热控制）三个社区追踪世界模型功能解剖学的演变。

Result: 发现模型降阶社区贡献了可验证性（先验解析误差界）、物理基础和极端数据效率；学习型世界模型贡献了非线性表示能力、可迁移性和长预测时域。两者互补但缺乏统一。

Conclusion: 安全关键系统（电力、热力、过程控制）中部署世界模型的核心障碍是可验证性而非预测精度，需要发展物理基础的、可验证的世界模型以统一两条研究路线。

Abstract: World models -- compressed latent representations of an environment that support action-conditioned prediction and planning -- are typically presented as a product of modern self-supervised learning. This paper argues that the functional anatomy of a world model was independently developed, deployed, and formally analyzed decades earlier in the model-order-reduction (MOR) and control literature, under different names and for a different purpose: the real-time operation of physical systems. We trace the anatomy across three communities. Low-dimensional models of turbulence built on proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) supplied latent dynamics learned from data of a chaotic environment; eigenface methods in early computer vision supplied the encoder-decoder half, including a primitive runtime validity check; and measurement-based POD frameworks for facility thermal control assembled the complete loop -- POD coefficients as latent state, parametric dependence on actuator setpoints as action conditioning, modal reconstruction as decoding, and, critically, a priori analytical error bounds as a verification layer that certified when the model's predictions could be trusted in closed loop. We then examine what each tradition possesses that the other lacks: MOR contributes verification, physical grounding, and extreme data efficiency; learned world models contribute nonlinear representation, transferability, and horizon. We argue that the outstanding obstacle to deploying world models in systems that cannot fail -- power, thermal, process control -- is not predictive fidelity but verifiability, and we outline a research agenda for physics-grounded, verifiable world models that unifies the two lineages.

</details>


### [116] [Transition Information Density: Morphological Trajectories, Synesthetic Perception, and Structured Interpolation in Neural Training (or: The Synesthetic AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03210)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出过渡信息密度（TID）和位置同一性（Positional Identity）两个概念，通过字素-颜色联觉、Synesthesia Grid算法及四条件训练实验，证明基于结构化插值的中间状态训练能显著降低语音/语言和语义描述领域的本征维度，但视觉和跨模态领域无此效应，揭示了模态边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习训练将数据表示为离散端点对，忽略了端点间空间的结构信息，本文旨在通过引入TID和Positional Identity来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于三个实证背景（字素-颜色联觉、Synesthesia Grid边界轮廓变形算法、四种表征媒介的四条件训练实验），采用结构化插值训练探针，对比四种条件（C3: 结构化位置同一性 vs C2: 体积匹配控制），使用TwoNN分析等方法评估本征维度。

Result: C3条件下语音/语言领域本征维度3.33（vs C2的10.81），语义描述领域4.59（vs C2的8.67）；视觉和跨模态无此效应；固定N=50比较确认位置同一性结构而非样本数量驱动效果；分辨率N与表征丰富度单调相关；Pooled TwoNN显示视觉空间全局折叠（0.075）而语音空间全局一致（0.977）。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了TID和Positional Identity的形式化定义、九指标形状表征框架、四条件实验设计，证实了中间状态结构信息对表征学习的关键作用，并揭示了不同模态间的边界条件。

Abstract: Standard machine learning training presents data as discrete endpoint pairs, omitting the structure of the space between them. This paper introduces Transition Information Density (TID) -- the information content recoverable from structured intermediate states between categorically distinct training endpoints -- and Positional Identity, the defined location of an intermediate state on the A-to-B continuum. Both constructs are grounded in three empirical contexts: grapheme-color synesthesia, the Synesthesia Grid (a boundary-contour morphing algorithm instantiating TID in visual morphological space), and a four-condition training experiment across four representational mediums. Probes trained on structured interpolation at defined Positional Identities (C3) exhibit substantially lower intrinsic dimensionality than volume-matched controls (C2) in Phonetic/Linguistic (C3: 3.33 vs. C2: 10.81) and Semantic Description (C3: 4.59 vs. C2: 8.67) mediums. Visual and cross-modal mediums do not show this effect, establishing a modality boundary condition. A fixed-N=50 comparison confirms that Positional Identity structure, not sample count, drives the effect. Resolution N scales monotonically with representational richness. Pooled TwoNN analysis reveals globally collapsed representations in visual space (0.075) and globally consistent representations in phonetic space (0.977). The paper contributes a formal definition of TID and Positional Identity, a nine-metric shape characterization framework, and a four-condition experimental design isolating trajectory structure, data volume, and Positional Identity as distinct factors.

</details>


### [117] [Joint distribution of upstream runoff governs downstream river-discharge prediction uncertainty in distributed ML models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03217)
*Karan Ruparell,Tristan Hascoet,Takemasa Miyoshi,Kieran M. R. Hunt,Hannah L. Cloke,Christel Prudhomme,Florian Pappenberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了从集总式概率水文预测转向分布式模型时，必须对上游径流生成的联合分布进行联合采样，否则独立采样会导致下游集合预报分散度严重不足，并提出分位数匹配策略作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来生成式机器学习方法推动了概率流预报的发展，但这些方法局限于集总式模型（直接预测流域出口）。与此同时，确定性LSTM径流模型正越来越多地应用于网格或子流域尺度，并通过河道汇流网络生成空间连续、物理一致的流场。本文认为，将概率预报从集总式扩展到分布式模型时，出现了一个新的需求：必须对上游径流生成的联合分布进行联合采样，否则独立采样会平均化不确定性，导致下游集合预报过于集中。

Method: 以日本为案例，训练两个概率性子流域尺度径流LSTM模型，并通过Hayami汇流方案进行河道汇流。对比两种策略：1) 随机匹配上游集合成员（独立采样）；2) 分位数匹配策略（保证上游集合成员在分位数上对齐）。以直接集总式流域出口预报作为参考基准。

Result: 随机匹配上游集合成员会导致下游集合预报严重欠分散（under-dispersed），而简单的分位数匹配策略能恢复与直接集总式基准模型相当的下游集合分散度。

Conclusion: 从集总式转向分布式概率水文预报时，必须明确关注径流不确定性的空间联合结构，否则独立采样会导致不确定性被低估。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification of hydrological predictions is necessary to inform operational decisions. Recent generative machine-learning methods have advanced probabilistic streamflow prediction, but have remained confined to lumped models that predict a basin outlet directly. At the same time, deterministic LSTM runoff models are increasingly applied at grid or catchment scale and routed through river networks to produce spatially continuous, physically consistent discharge fields. This technical note argues that moving probabilistic prediction from lumped to distributed models introduces a specific new requirement: the joint distribution of upstream runoff generation must be sampled jointly. In lumped inference, the model predicts the outlet distribution directly and can modulate spread from basin attributes. In distributed inference, downstream discharge is obtained by routing many upstream runoff predictions, so independent local sampling averages uncertainty away. Using Japan as a case study, we train two probabilistic basin-scale runoff LSTMs and route their runoff through a Hayami routing scheme. Randomly matching upstream ensemble members produces severely under-dispersed downstream ensembles, whereas a simple quantile matching strategy restores much of the spread of the direct basin-scale reference. The shift from lumped to distributed probabilistic hydrology therefore requires explicit attention to the spatial joint structure of runoff uncertainty.

</details>


### [118] [PhenoNEST: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Ontology-Aware Multimodal Plant Phenotyping and Trait Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03245)
*Jayant Ghadge,Soumyashree Kar,Surya S. Durbha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态细粒度知识图谱框架，通过RDF-typing、PlantDeBERTa本体对齐和视觉-语言模型注意力软图，将非结构化的田间文本和RGB图像转化为结构化时空图谱，实现小麦基因型-表型互作监测，在WisWheat数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量植物表型数据常困于非结构化文本和孤立的RGB图像中，缺乏语义关联，难以有效监测基因型-表型随时间及实验的互作关系。

Method: 1) 从噪声田间笔记中提取实体和关系，通过RDF-typing将唯一实例转化为层次化类实体，动态构建KG；2) 使用PlantDeBERTa将图节点与标准化本体(PO/RO/WTO)对齐；3) 采用VLM+小麦分割ViT生成注意力软图，将KG实体与图像像素关联；4) 引入Plant_Obs_Id中心观测节点实现多模态子图的时间连接。

Result: 在500个WisWheat样本上，使用Pointing Game准确率、VWSD和排序指标进行评估，成功将复杂田间观测映射为结构化图谱，实现田间笔记自动审计、时间应力监测和空间性状定位。

Conclusion: 该神经符号方法有效弥合了非结构化文本与结构化知识之间的语义鸿沟，为小麦育种提供了多模态、时空可追溯的表型监测工具。

Abstract: High-throughput plant phenotyping generates valuable data that often remains trapped in unstructured text and isolated RGB images. To bridge this semantic gap, we propose a framework for constructing a multimodal granular Knowledge Graph (KG) to monitor genotype-phenotype interactions across time and experiments. In this work, we focus on wheat Triticum aestivum as a representative target crop to validate our methodology across complex canopy environments. Our pipeline first distills noisy field notes to extract entities and relations, dynamically constructing the KG by converting unique instances into hierarchical class entities via RDF-typing. These graph nodes are then aligned with standardized ontologies (PO, RO, WTO) using PlantDeBERTa. To visually ground the constructed graph, a Vision-Language Model paired with a wheat-segmentation ViT generates attention-based softmaps, linking specific KG entities directly to image pixels. We introduce a central observation node Plant_Obs_Id to connect these multimodal subgraphs temporally. Evaluated on 500 curated WisWheat samples using Pointing Game accuracy, Visual Word Sense Disambiguation (VWSD), and rank-based metrics, our neuro-symbolic approach successfully maps complex field observations to a structured graph. This enables automated field note auditing, temporal stress monitoring, and precise spatial trait localization for wheat breeders.

</details>


### [119] [Unbiased Alignment for Large Language Models with Noisy Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03248)
*Jialiang Wang,Xianming Liu,Xiong Zhou,Hui Liu,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无偏奖励模型（URM）和无偏直接偏好优化（UDPO）损失函数，从理论上解决了偏好数据集中噪声导致的模型训练偏差问题，无需干净标注即可实现无偏对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF和DPO方法在处理真实世界偏好数据集中的显著噪声时表现脆弱，需要从理论上解决噪声导致的训练偏差问题。

Method: 提出URM损失和UDPO损失，通过数学修正偏好噪声引起的扭曲，实现无需干净监督即可从噪声数据集中进行无偏模型训练。

Result: 理论分析证明了方法的噪声容忍性、参数向下兼容性和分类校准性；实验表明方法优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效应对偏好噪声，实现大语言模型与人类偏好的无偏对齐。

Abstract: The alignment of large language models with human preferences is commonly achieved through Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback or Direct Preference Optimization. However, these methods are vulnerable to the significant noise prevalent in real-world preference datasets. To address this critical issue, we present a theoretical framework for unbiased alignment, introducing the Unbiased Reward Model (URM) loss and the Unbiased Direct Preference Optimization (UDPO) loss. By mathematically correcting the distortion induced by preference noise, our novel objectives enable unbiased model training directly from noisy datasets, without requiring clean ground-truth supervision. We provide rigorous theoretical analyses demonstrating that our methods are noise-tolerant, parameter downward compatible, and classification-calibrated. Comprehensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our approaches outperform state-of-the-art baselines. Code available at: https://github.com/cswjl/unbiased-alignment.

</details>


### [120] [From Global to Local: Efficient Regional Weather Downscaling with Global Weather Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03279)
*Wiktor Kamzela,Jakub Kubiak,Adam Dobosz,Jędrzej Miczke,Anatol Kaczmarek,Piotr Wyrwiński,Wojciech Stefaniak,Wojciech Kotłowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预训练气象基础模型的降尺度框架，通过在潜在空间中添加轻量级多尺度预测头，实现对全球预报的区域精细化，在计算成本极低的情况下达到比传统数值天气预报更优的精度，并优于标准图像超分辨率方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限区域模型计算成本高，而现有学习方法通常将问题简化为超分辨率，忽视了不同尺度间的统计和物理不匹配问题。

Method: 以预训练天气模型骨干网络为基础，在其潜在空间中添加轻量级多尺度预测头，学习全球预报的区域精细化；使用区域数值模拟作为训练目标，并在网格数据集和地面气象站观测数据上进行评估。

Result: 在大多数指标上优于传统数值天气预报，计算成本极低；基于全球预训练气象基础模型的潜在空间进行降尺度，效果优于标准的图像超分辨率方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效利用预训练模型的潜在空间实现高效、高精度的区域降尺度，无需重新训练骨干网络，且能分析全球再分析、区域模拟和地面观测之间的系统偏差。

Abstract: Accurate regional weather prediction requires resolving fine-scale structure while remaining consistent with global dynamics. Traditional limited area models rely on computationally expensive simulations, while many learning-based approaches frame the problem as super-resolution, overlooking statistical and physical mismatches across scales. We propose a foundation-model-driven downscaling framework that learns regional refinements of global forecasts by augmenting a pretrained weather model backbone with lightweight, multi-scale prediction heads operating directly in its latent space. Despite being trained on substantially coarser inputs, the pretrained backbone supports regional adaptation at resolutions corresponding to a two-order-of-magnitude increase in grid-cell resolution, without the need for retraining. The proposed approach uses regional numerical simulations as training targets and is evaluated not only against gridded datasets but also against ground-based weather station observations, enabling analysis of systematic biases between global reanalysis, regional simulations, and in-situ weather station observations. Our experiments show improved accuracy in comparison to NWP on most of the metrics at the fraction of computational cost. Moreover, we observe that building on a latent space of globally pre-trained weather foundation model offers better downscaling capabilities than the standard image-based super-resolution approaches.

</details>


### [121] [A harmonised dataset for Earth system foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03298)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WorldTensor是一个将数百个环境和社会经济变量统一到标准化0.25°空间网格和年度时间框架上的全球协调数据集，旨在为地球系统基础模型提供多模态训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球系统基础模型主要基于物理气候和天气数据训练，缺乏对人类系统的表征；缺乏一个统一的全球训练资源来整合气候、陆地、海洋、冰冻圈、基础设施、灾害和社会经济数据，阻碍了真正多模态地球系统基础模型的发展。

Method: 将不同原生分辨率和投影的输入数据重新网格化；将点和矢量数据栅格化为空间有意义的网格场；协调从每日观测到稀疏多年社会经济快照的不同时间覆盖范围；所有输出以NetCDF格式发布，配有标准化坐标、变量元数据和通用CF元数据约定。

Result: 构建了一个整合再分析产品、遥感数据、排放清单、土地利用重建、水文观测、基础设施和灾害数据集以及社会经济指标的全球协调数据集，对齐到标准化0.25°空间网格和年度时间框架。

Conclusion: WorldTensor为训练和评估能够学习环境与人类系统耦合动力学的行星尺度基础模型提供了可复现的资源。

Abstract: Foundation models for Earth systems have so far been trained primarily on physical climate and weather data, with limited representation of the human systems that both drive and respond to environmental change. The lack of a unified global training resource that combines climate, land, ocean, cryosphere, infrastructure, hazards, and socioeconomic data on a common grid hinders progress toward truly multimodal Earth system foundation models. We present WorldTensor, a harmonised global dataset that aligns hundreds of environmental and socioeconomic variables to a standardised 0.25$^\circ$ spatial grid and annual temporal framework. WorldTensor integrates reanalysis products, remote sensing, emissions inventories, land use reconstructions, hydrological observations, infrastructure and hazard datasets, and socioeconomic indicators within a single representation designed for machine learning workflows. To build the dataset, we regridded inputs across heterogeneous native resolutions and projections, rasterised point and vector datasets into spatially meaningful gridded fields, and reconciled temporal coverages ranging from daily observations to sparse multiyear socioeconomic snapshots. All outputs are distributed as NetCDF files with standardised coordinates, variable metadata, and a common CF metadata convention. WorldTensor provides a reproducible resource for training and evaluating foundation models that learn coupled dynamics across environmental and human systems at planetary scale.

</details>


### [122] [Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG) Beyond the Euclidean Paradigm, with Application to Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03329)
*Bing Cheng,Yi-Shuai Niu,Howell Tong,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统计有意义的几何（SMG）框架，将过参数化模型的参数空间提升为无限维Orlicz统计流形，利用微分纤维丛和Ehresmann联络形式动态滤除垂直规范噪声，理论上界定了生成幻觉并完全消除灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一致收敛界和经验风险最小化在过度参数化模型（如大语言模型、生物序列网络）中失效，优化景观存在平坦垂直规范谷，导致经典泛化指标无效，并引发生成幻觉和灾难性遗忘等严重病理。

Method: 将确定性参数模型提升为无限维非参数Orlicz统计流形；将总状态空间建模为微分纤维丛(M,B,π,V,H,ω)；定义Ehresmann联络1-形式ω作为动态几何滤波器，剥离垂直规范噪声（SID），沿严格非退化水平分布（SVDχ）隔离学习轨迹。

Result: 证明在联络滤波预训练下，分布外预测方差严格以可识别商基流形B的有限直径为上界，建立生成幻觉的硬几何包含；通过将下游更新投影到历史水平承载的正交补上，证明灾难性遗忘的完全非渐近消除。

Conclusion: SMG用无坐标拓扑约束替代经验微调启发式，将高级微分几何与AI结构可靠性相衔接，为过参数化模型提供了严格的几何泛化理论框架。

Abstract: Conventional uniform convergence bounds and empirical risk minimization break down in massive over-parameterized models, such as large language transformers and biological sequence networks. With near-infinite unconstrained internal degrees of freedom, their optimization landscapes develop flat vertical gauge valleys, rendering classical generalization metrics vacuous and inducing severe pathologies, specifically generative hallucination and catastrophic forgetting. We introduce the Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG) framework, an information-geometric paradigm lifting deterministic parametric models into infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz statistical manifolds. Modeling the total state space as a differential fiber bundle ($\mathcal{M}, \mathcal{B}, π, \mathcal{V}, \mathcal{H}, ω$), we establish a Two-Fold Inference Paradigm. We formalize an Ehresmann connection 1-form $ω$ as a dynamic geometric filter that strips away vertical gauge noise (Structural Internal Directions, or SID) and isolates learning trajectories along the strictly non-degenerate horizontal distribution (Statistical Variational Directions, or SVD$χ$). We prove that under connection-filtered pre-training, out-of-distribution predictive variance is strictly upper-bounded by the finite diameter of the identifiable quotient base manifold $\mathcal{B}$, establishing a hard geometric containment of generative hallucinations. By projecting downstream updates onto the orthogonal complement of the historical horizontal carriage, we formalize the SMG Sequential Adaptation Flow, proving the total non-asymptotic elimination of catastrophic forgetting. SMG replaces empirical fine-tuning heuristics with coordinate-free topological constraints, bridging advanced differential geometry with structural reliability in AI.

</details>


### [123] [FedAvg for HAR: Exploring the Tradeoff Between Personalized and Generalization Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03334)
*Andrea De Luna,Susanna Peretti,Chiara Contoli,Alessandro Bogliolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习（FL）中模型个性化与泛化能力的权衡，通过设计多种测试场景（包括客户端数据变化模拟）评估FedAvg算法在人类活动识别（HAR）领域的表现。结果表明，虽然FedAvg相比传统集中学习在保持高泛化能力的同时具有更高的个性化程度，但在类分布变化等压力条件下，这一优势并不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中需要在模型的个性化（适配特定客户端）与泛化（通用性能）能力之间寻找平衡点，现有研究对这一权衡的理解尚不充分。

Method: 设计并实现了多种测试场景，用于评估和比较集中式、本地式和联邦式范式性能；设计了一个模拟客户端数据变化的场景；应用FedAvg算法于人类活动识别（HAR）领域进行实验。

Result: FedAvg算法在保持较高泛化能力的同时展现出更高的个性化程度；但在客户端类分布变化等压力条件下，其优势不再显著。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在个性化与泛化之间存在权衡，标准FedAvg在常规条件下表现良好，但面对数据分布偏移等挑战时仍需改进。

Abstract: The federated learning (FL) paradigm fosters distributed pervasive computing combined with artificial intelligence techniques, allowing for optimized data usage and improved mitigation of privacy concerns. Indeed, model training occurs on the client's local devices, and model parameters are subsequently shared with a centralized server. However, there is a need to find a tradeoff between models' personalization and generalization capabilities. In this paper, we design and implement several testing scenarios devoted to evaluating and comparing the centralized, local, and federated paradigm performances. We also design and implement a scenario that emulates a change in clients' data. We then present experimental results of the FedAvg algorithm applied to the Human Activity Recognition (HAR) domain to understand the trade-off between personalized and generalized accuracy. Results show that, although FedAvg confirms a higher degree of personalization capabilities while keeping a high degree of generalization with respect to the traditional centralized learning, this result is not so obvious under stressful conditions, such as when varying class distribution over clients.

</details>


### [124] [CSympNet-ID: conformal-symplectic map learning for linearly damped Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03339)
*Jiale Gong,Pengzhan Jin,Dongyang Kuang,Lu Li,Yifa Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CSympNet-ID框架，通过显式构造离散共形辛结构学习阻尼动力系统的一步流映射，在数据稀缺、高维等场景下优于非结构化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离散观测中学习耗散动力学对于可靠的长时预测和物理上有意义的参数识别至关重要，而线性阻尼哈密顿系统的精确流是共形辛的，现有方法难以保证离散共形辛性质的精确满足。

Method: 将精确辛神经网络核心与显式对角缩放层组合，缩放因子通过标量阻尼率参数的指数函数参数化，保证正定性和可解释性；建立了共形辛映射的缩放共轭分解，并推导了点态密度结果。

Result: 在不规则步长阻尼振荡器、阻尼弹簧质量链、阻尼非线性立方振荡器及高维扩展实验中，CSympNet-ID在数据稀缺场景、目标收缩律恢复和高维测试中表现最优。

Conclusion: 通过强制构造精确离散共形辛性，该框架在长时预测和物理参数辨识上优于非结构化基线，尤其适用于数据有限和高维耗散系统。

Abstract: Learning dissipative dynamics from discrete observations is essential for reliable long-horizon prediction and physically meaningful parameter identification. For linearly damped Hamiltonian systems, the exact flow is generally not symplectic but conformally symplectic, contracting the canonical symplectic form by a scalar factor that reflects the net dissipation. We propose Conformal Symplectic Networks with damping identification (CSympNet-ID), a discrete-time map-learning framework that learns the one-step flow map directly from snapshot pairs while enforcing exact discrete conformal symplecticity by construction, without penalty terms or projection. The architecture composes an exact symplectic neural core with explicit diagonal scaling layers whose factors are parameterized exponentially by a scalar damping-rate parameter, thereby guaranteeing positivity and interpretability of the learned dissipation factor. We establish a scaling-conjugacy factorization for conformal symplectic maps and derive a pointwise-in-step density result for CSympNet-ID. We evaluate an irregular-step damped oscillator, a damped spring-mass chain, a damped nonlinear cubic oscillator, and additional high-dimensional extensions. CSympNet-ID gives the most favorable overall results among the compared models in the reported experiments, particularly in data-scarce regimes, target contraction-law recovery, and high-dimensional tests where unstructured baselines degrade rapidly.

</details>


### [125] [The Multiscale Single-Index Model: A Stylized Model for Hierarchical Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03347)
*Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多尺度单指标模型（MSIM）进行了精细的Wiener混沌分析，利用Edgeworth展开揭示了非线性引起的扰动层次结构，证明了在线SGD在相关目标上能以与线性模型相同的样本复杂度实现高概率恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱方法（如张量展开）由于扰动项精度不足，无法分析基于反向传播的梯度方法。本文旨在弥补这一缺陷，为MSIM中的梯度下降训练提供精确的理论刻画。

Method: 采用Edgeworth展开对Wiener混沌进行精细分析：在一阶混沌中得到$d^{-q/2}$尺度的有限秩层次；在高阶混沌中，在非抵消条件下，平衡展平展现出阶梯状奇异值平台（尺度$d^{-ρ/2}$，多重度$d^ρ$）。结合慢Hermite能量尾条件，推导浅层网络近似下界和在线SGD的样本复杂度。

Result: 证明了在线SGD在相关目标上，所有层以相同时间尺度演化时，仅需$n = \widetilde{O}(d^{K-1})$个样本即可达到$1 - o_d(1)$的恢复率，与线性对应模型的样本复杂度一致。

Conclusion: 深度架构在多尺度特征学习中具有层级优势；MSIM中梯度方法的恢复效率与线性方法相同，验证了深度学习的样本效率优势。

Abstract: We consider the Multiscale Single-Index Model (MSIM), first introduced in \cite{oymak2021learning}, as a stylized model for hierarchical learning with \emph{scale separation}. Each layer extracts a shared single-index feature at one physical scale and passes it to the next, thus defining a tractable setting in which to study how deep architectures learn multiscale representations. Under non-degeneracy and delocalization assumptions on the link function and planted features respectively, for fixed depth $K$ and local scale $d$, the first Wiener chaos of the target behaves as a perturbed spiked tensor, where the perturbation of order $d^{-1/2}$ comes from the non-linearity -- revealing the MSIM as a natural non-linear analogue of the Tensor PCA model \cite{montanari2014statistical}. While this perturbative picture is sufficient to enable efficient spectral recovery based on Tensor unfolding (as already observed in \cite{oymak2021learning}), it is not precise enough for the analysis of backpropagation gradient-based methods. In this work, we address this limitation by performing a fine-grained analysis of the Wiener chaos using Edgeworth expansions. In the first chaos, this gives a finite-rank hierarchy at scales $d^{-q/2}$. In higher chaoses, balanced flattenings exhibit staircase singular-value plateaus of size $d^{-ρ/2}$ and multiplicity $d^ρ$ under a natural higher-chaos non-cancellation condition. Using this higher-chaos structure, and under an additional slow Hermite-energy tail condition, we first establish shallow-network approximation lower bounds, quantifying the benefit of depth in this model. Next, and most importantly, we prove that online SGD on the correlation objective, where all layers evolve in the same timescale, achieves $1 - o_d(1)$ recovery with $n = \widetilde{O}( d^{K-1})$ samples, recovering the same sample complexity as in the linear counterpart.

</details>


### [126] [PedestrianDiffusion: Multimodal Generative Denoising and Dense State Estimation for Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03349)
*I-Hao Lu,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PedestrianDiffusion，一种基于频域的多模态生成框架，将密集6D状态估计重构为连续条件去噪过程，通过谱协方差约束和零样本语义条件实现鲁棒的惯性导航，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级惯性导航精度受限于MEMS随机噪声，传统确定性神经网络存在"估计抖动"问题，在高频运动保真度与数值稳定性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 将6D状态估计建模为频域中的连续条件扩散去噪过程；2) 通过频域操作限制谱协方差作为数学预条件子稳定逆向扩散轨迹；3) 提出零样本语义条件机制，利用视觉-语言嵌入作为类别先验，泛化到异质传感器噪声；4) 采用单步确定性概率流ODE求解器(T=1)实现高效异步批处理轨迹精化。

Result: 在OxIOD、RIDI、RoNIN和TLIO基准上达到SOTA性能，对脉冲扰动和耦合6D运动学漂移展现出前所未有的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代神经惯性测量单元(N-IMU)提供了严格的算法蓝图，验证了生成式架构在边缘硬件上进行异步批处理轨迹精化的可行性。

Abstract: The accuracy of consumer-grade inertial navigation is bottlenecked by the stochastic noise of Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS). Traditional deterministic neural architectures often succumb to ``estimation jittering,'' sacrificing high-frequency kinematic fidelity for numerical stability. We propose PedestrianDiffusion, a multimodal spectral-domain generative framework reformulating dense 6D state estimation as a continuous conditional denoising process. By operating in the frequency domain, our formulation bounds the spectral covariance, acting as a mathematical preconditioner to stabilize the reverse diffusion trajectory. Furthermore, we introduce a zero-shot semantic conditioning mechanism leveraging vision-language embeddings as categorical priors to generalize across heterogeneous sensor noise profiles. To address the computational intractability of generative tracking, we deploy a single-step deterministic probability flow ODE solver ($T=1$). This yields high-capacity asynchronous batch trajectory refinement, establishing the viability of generative architectures for asynchronous batch trajectory refinement on edge hardware. Extensive evaluations on the OxIOD, RIDI, RoNIN, and TLIO benchmarks demonstrate that PedestrianDiffusion achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting unprecedented robustness to impulse perturbations and coupled 6D kinematic drift. This work provides a rigorous algorithmic blueprint for next-generation Neural Inertial Measurement Units (N-IMUs).

</details>


### [127] [Amortising Bayesian Experimental Design for Sequential Information Gathering in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03426)
*Jakob Hartmann,James Harvey,Jhonathan Navott,Erik Y. Wang,Luckeciano C. Melo,Flaviu Cipcigan,Cheng Zhang,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASIG方法，通过将贝叶斯实验设计（BED）摊销到大语言模型策略中，利用多轮GRPO和期望信息增益奖励进行微调。在20 Questions任务上，ASIG使7B基础模型成功率翻倍，推理成本降低25倍以上；在MediQ医学诊断基准上，ASIG提升了信息获取性能，且策略可迁移至分布外场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备强大的推理和世界知识，但在需要多轮交互的序列决策场景中，难以有效收集信息。现有方法如BED-LLM虽有效但推理成本高，缺乏高效的信息获取策略微调手段。

Method: 提出Amortised Sequential Information Gathering (ASIG)，将贝叶斯实验设计（BED）摊销到LLM策略中。具体采用多轮扩展的Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO)，以期望信息增益（Expected Information Gain）作为奖励信号进行微调，实现高效的信息获取策略学习。

Result: 在20 Questions任务上，ASIG使7B基础模型成功率翻倍，推理成本相较BED-LLM基线降低25倍以上；在训练时未见过的MediQ医学诊断基准上，ASIG在7B规模下提升了信息获取性能，表明策略可分布外迁移。

Conclusion: 将BED摊销到LLM策略中是一种有效且计算高效的序列信息获取方法，能够在保持低推理成本的同时显著提升信息收集能力，且学到的策略具有一定的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning and world-knowledge capabilities, yet often struggle to gather information effectively across the multi-turn interactions required in sequential decision-making settings. We introduce Amortised Sequential Information Gathering (ASIG), a fine-tuning approach that amortises Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) into LLM policies via a multi-turn extension of Group Relative Policy Optimisation with an Expected Information Gain reward. Evaluated on the 20 Questions task, ASIG more than doubles the success rate of the 7B base model and reduces inference cost by over $25\times$ relative to BED-LLM, a competitive inference-time baseline. Applied to MediQ, a medical diagnosis benchmark unseen during training, ASIG improves information-seeking performance at the 7B scale, suggesting that the learned strategies can transfer out of distribution. Our findings show that amortising BED into LLM policies provides an effective and computationally efficient approach to sequential information gathering.

</details>


### [128] [How Much of the Routing Gap Is Real? Decomposing the Router-to-Oracle Gap into Reproducible Specialist Advantage and Single-Draw Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03436)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM路由中的“单次采样oracle”本质是伯努利变量而非确定性属性，通过分解期望oracle为可复现优势项与噪声项，证明噪声项无法被任何单次提交路由器消除但可通过测试时采样（best-of-K）恢复，并在LLMRouterBench（33模型，39万实例）上量化噪声占比可达近半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由评估使用单次解码（单个正确性标签）定义的oracle，但随机解码下该oracle仅是伯努利抽样结果而非稳定属性，导致路由器的性能上界被系统性高估或误估。需要从结构上厘清哪些gap是真正的模型选择优势、哪些是随机噪声。

Method: 1) 期望oracle分解：O^exp = O^repro + Δ，其中O^repro为可复现的单次提交优势，Δ为单次提交选择下限（不可被单次路由器消除）；2) 恢复不对称性证明：Δ无法被任何单次提交路由器闭合，但可通过测试时采样（在已选模型上做best-of-K，使用oracle自身预算）消除；3) 噪声估计：在LLMRouterBench上通过k≥20重采样，使用单侧、依赖校正和猜测下限校正的边界方法估计噪声份额。

Result: 单次抽样的噪声在gap中占显著 minorities——在未饱和基准上更大，在最难查询（无模型可靠）上可达近半；多数gap仍来自可恢复的专家优势。单次解码定义的oracle会因thin-support union膨胀而高估模型召回失败。

Conclusion: 路由评估必须考虑随机解码的噪声影响；测试时采样（best-of-K）可在不增加新路由器的前提下恢复oracle的噪声下限；应使用多采样oracle评估协议来获得更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) promises better quality at lower cost, motivated by the reported gap between learned routers and a per-instance oracle. But that oracle is computed from a single correctness label per (query, model), so under stochastic decoding it is one Bernoulli draw, not a reproducible property. We recast the question structurally: the expected per-instance oracle decomposes as $O^{\exp}=O^{\mathrm{repro}}+Δ$, into reproducible single-commit headroom $O^{\mathrm{repro}}$ and a non-negative single-commit selection floor $Δ$. Our main result is a recoverability asymmetry: this floor is closed by no single-commit router, yet is recovered by test-time sampling -- best-of-$K$ on the committed model, at the oracle's own budget, dominates the independent-pool single-draw oracle. The cap needs no cross-model independence; we prove it with the exact decomposition and noise-share bounds that shrink as the budget grows. The procedure adds no new router, only resampling. The floor's magnitude is a prospective, conservative localization, not an audit: our primary target LLMRouterBench (33 models, 391,645 instances) defines its oracle as a per-query union over single $T=0.2$ generations -- by construction a union of stochastic single draws. Since $O^{\mathrm{repro}}$ is non-identifiable from the released $k=1$ matrix, we estimate the noise share by fresh $k\ge20$ resampling under one-sided, dependence- and guessing-floor-corrected bounds, recasting 'model-recall failure' as thin-support union inflation. On a controlled open-model re-generation, single-draw noise is a substantial minority of the gap -- larger on an unsaturated benchmark, approaching half on the hardest queries where no model is reliable -- while the majority remains recoverable specialist advantage. We release a multi-sample oracle evaluation protocol for routing benchmarks.

</details>


### [129] [No Time Like the Present: Agentic Test-Time Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03441)
*Yanbo Wang,Jinhua Hao,Yuze Shi,Kun Yuan,Ming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT)，一种针对多轮智能体交互场景的连续测试时训练方法，通过基于n-gram重复检测的token级重加权机制来缓解智能体在长轨迹中的性能退化问题，并在ALFWorld和SWE-bench Lite上分别取得最高5.0和4.9个百分点的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时间交互轨迹中会出现性能退化，表现为重访已探索状态、重复失败动作以及丢失先前有效的策略。现有的测试时训练方法主要针对单次输入进行一次性适应，无法应对多轮交互场景中持续变化的任务状态。

Method: aTTM的核心是一种token级重加权方法，通过检测当前更新文本中是否存在来自先前更新的重复n-gram，对重复出现的token降低损失权重，同时对新颖token保持完整权重。为支持在线更新，系统基于vLLM的运行时LoRA API构建了并发服务架构。

Result: 在ALFWorld上成功率提升最高5.0个百分点，在SWE-bench Lite上提升最高4.9个百分点。系统开销控制为无TTT成本的1.9倍。性能提升集中在模型已具备任务能力但因长轨迹而漂移的场景，表明aTTT主要作用是保持现有能力而非教授新能力。

Conclusion: 连续TTT在智能体多轮交互中是有效的，但需要处理自训练循环中的漂移问题。aTTt通过检测更新文本重复性来缓解这一困境，其增益主要来源于对已有能力的稳定维持而非能力的新增。

Abstract: LLM agents often degrade over long episodes: as trajectories grow, they revisit explored states, repeat failed actions, and lose strategies that previously worked. Test-time training (TTT) offers a way to adapt model weights to the evolving task state, but existing LLM TTT methods largely adapt once to a fixed input. We study continuous TTT in multi-turn agent episodes, where each update changes the policy that generates later training text. This creates a self-training loop that helps when new trajectory information appears, but can amplify drift when the agent gets stuck and repeatedly trains on similar text. We find that update-text repetition distinguishes these regimes and introduce Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT), a token-level reweighting method that downweights the loss on tokens appearing in repeated $n$-grams from prior updates while leaving novel tokens fully weighted. To run such updates inside live episodes, we build a concurrent serving system using vLLM's runtime LoRA API, limiting overhead to 1.9$\times$ the no-TTT cost. aTTT improves success by up to 5.0 points on ALFWorld and 4.9 points on SWE-bench Lite. The gains concentrate where models already have task competence but drift over long trajectories, suggesting that aTTT mainly preserves existing competence rather than teaching new abilities.

</details>


### [130] [Best-of-Better-$N$: Generating Pre-Aligned Responses with In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03453)
*Eric Lei,Hsiang Hsu,Chun-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推理时对齐方法受限于参考LLM响应质量的问题，提出Best-of-Better-N（BoBN）方法，通过基于检索和重写的高奖励示例进行上下文学习，使采样分布向高奖励区域偏移，在安全对齐和数学推理任务上提升了响应质量并降低了所需采样数量N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法（如Best-of-N）的性能受限于参考语言模型本身生成响应的质量上限——若参考模型对高奖励响应分配的概率极低，则任何选择策略都无法找到对齐输出。因此需要一种方法在不依赖额外训练的前提下提升生成响应本身的质量。

Method: 提出Best-of-Better-N（BoBN）框架：首先从高奖励示例库中检索与输入查询和任务相关的示例；然后通过重写步骤（restyling）让参考LLM将检索到的响应重写为目标任务的格式和风格；最后将这些重写后的示例作为上下文，引导参考LLM的采样分布向高奖励区域偏移。

Result: 在安全对齐和数学推理基准上的实验表明，BoBN在固定N时能获得更好的性能，且达到目标性能所需的N更小。同时从理论上证明了上下文学习如何使预训练Transformer的输出分布向高奖励区域偏移，并带来可证明的收益。

Conclusion: BoBN通过上下文学习有效提升了推理时对齐方法的响应质量，突破了传统Best-of-N方法受限于参考模型生成能力的瓶颈，在固定采样预算下取得了更优的对齐效果。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-$N$, offer a flexible alternative to training-based alignment by using reward models to select high-quality responses generated by a reference LLM. However, the efficacy of these methods is inherently limited by the response quality: if the reference LLM assigns negligible probability to high-reward responses, no selection strategy will succeed in finding aligned outputs. In this work, we propose Best-of-Better-$N$ (BoBN), an in context learning-based generation framework to address this challenge. Our method utilizes retrieval from high-reward examples relevant to the input query and task. Crucially, we introduce a restyling step where retrieved responses are rewritten by the reference LLM to align with the target task's format and style. These restyled examples are used in-context to shift the sampling distribution toward the high-reward region. We analytically characterize how in-context learning shifts the output distribution of pretrained transformers toward the high-reward region, resulting in provable benefits on the target task. We then evaluate BoBN on safety alignment and mathematical reasoning benchmarks across several reference LLMs. BoBN's higher-quality responses enable better performance to be achieved when the number of responses $N$ is fixed, and smaller $N$ required to achieve a target performance.

</details>


### [131] [Towards Diverse and Comprehensive Benchmarks for Mutual Information Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03487)
*Alberto Foresti,Ivan Butakov,Alexander Tolmachev,Giulio Franzese,Alexey Frolov,Pietro Michiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于copula理论的互信息估计统一基准框架，包含两个互补的测试族，系统评估了非参数、判别式和生成式三类估计器，发现不存在通用最优方法，不同设置下各类估计器各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互信息估计基准仅评估简化低维分布，缺乏对复杂真实数据性能的系统评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于统一的copula理论视角构建基准框架，提出两类互补测试：(1) copula优先族：通过合成和流式变换系统变化真实MI、维度和边际复杂度；(2) 边际优先族：将真实图像数据与受控依赖结构结合，扩展经典同类别配对范式。

Result: 不存在通用最优估计器；每类估计器（非参数、判别式、生成式）在特定设置下均可系统性地优于其他所有估计器。

Conclusion: 通过分析各类估计器的优势案例，识别了基本估计障碍并提出了更有效针对这些局限性的新测试方法。

Abstract: Mutual information (MI) estimation is a central problem in machine learning and statistics; however, existing benchmarks typically evaluate estimators on simplified, low-dimensional distributions, leaving their performance on complex, realistic data largely unexplored. We address this gap with a comprehensive benchmarking framework grounded in a unified copula-theoretic perspective that subsumes existing benchmarks as special cases. Within this framework, we propose two complementary families of tests: a copula-first family that systematically varies ground-truth MI, dimensionality, and marginal complexity using synthetic and flow-based transformations; and a marginals-first family that couples real-world image data with controlled dependency structures, extending the classic same-class-pairing paradigm. We use this suite to extensively evaluate three classes of estimators: non-parametric, discriminative, and generative. Contrary to prevailing assumptions, our results indicate that there is no universal winner: each category can systematically outperform all other estimators under specific setups. By analyzing these cases, we identify fundamental estimation barriers and propose new tests that more effectively stress these specific limitations. We share the open source code at https://github.com/VanessB/mutinfo.

</details>


### [132] [A Near-Linear-Time Solver for Graph $p$-Laplacian Semi-Supervised Learning via Continuation in $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03503)
*Oren E. Livne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非线性p-Laplacian流的半监督学习算法，通过阻尼弦-牛顿延拓法在参数p上求解，解决了传统p=2方法在标签稀缺时的退化问题，实现了近线性时间复杂度的可扩展求解器，在大规模图上比直接分解快1.5–14倍，且精度显著优于p=2方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于p=2的图半监督学习在标签稀少且维度d≥2时，随着无标签数据增加，估计会退化为近似常数函数（Nadler-Srebro-Zhou指出），这正是SSL最需要发挥作用的场景。而克服该退化需要采用非线性p-Laplacian能量且p>d，但现有求解器在大规模图上效率低下，无法利用近线性Laplacian求解器。

Method: 将p-Laplacian SSL重写为源形式非线性Laplacian流Bρ_p(B^T x)=b，采用阻尼弦-牛顿延拓法在p上连续推进求解。每次线性化系统保持良态，可委托给近线性Laplacian求解引擎处理。

Result: 在规模化图族上，每族经验墙钟时间为m^{0.96}-m^{1.02}（默认近似Cholesky）；228个SuiteSparse图上的合并拟合为m^{1.19}（vs.直接分解的m^{1.45}）；可处理6.8×10^7条边的社交网络；Cholesky填充量达图非零元的10–280倍，而本方法为O(m)层次结构；比已发布的FCL求解器快1.5–14倍且精度匹配；MNIST 10-NN上p=3在每类1标签时达到64%准确率（p=2仅36%）。

Conclusion: 提出的方法成功弥合了p-Laplacian SSL理论与高效大规模实践之间的差距，使得非线性p-Laplacian求解在计算效率上可与线性Laplacian引擎相媲美，同时显著提升了标签稀少场景下的学习性能。

Abstract: Graph-based semi-supervised learning (SSL) propagates a few labels over a similarity graph by minimizing a Dirichlet-type energy. The standard quadratic ($p=2$) energy reduces to a single graph-Laplacian solve, but it degenerates exactly where SSL is most useful when labels are scarce: gathering more unlabeled data drives the $p=2$ estimate to a near-constant function whenever $d\ge2$ (Nadler-Srebro-Zhou). Well-posedness requires the nonlinear $p$-Laplacian energy with $p>d$. Existing solvers reduce this to a sequence of weighted Laplacian solves, but their reference implementations use a direct sparse factorization or ichol-preconditioned CG instead. Plugging a near-linear Laplacian solver is not straightforward: at large $p$ the conductance weights degenerate near flat-gradient edges, making the system nearly singular and causing stagnation without a damped outer iteration. We close this gap. Recasting $p$-Laplacian SSL as a source-form nonlinear Laplacian flow $Bρ_p(B^\top x)=b$ and solving by damped chord-Newton continuation in $p$, every linearized system stays well-conditioned and can be delegated to a near-linear Laplacian engine. On size-scaled graph families the wall-clock is empirically $m^{0.96}$-$m^{1.02}$ per family (approximate Cholesky default), and a pooled fit across 228 SuiteSparse graphs gives $m^{1.19}$ vs.\ $m^{1.45}$ for direct factorization; the solver handles a $6.8\times10^7$-edge social network in minutes. Memory is the binding constraint: Cholesky fill reaches $10$-$280\times$ the graph nonzeros vs.\ our $O(m)$ hierarchy. Against the released FCL solver we are $1.5$-$14\times$ faster at matched accuracy. On MNIST $10$-NN, $p=3$ scores $64\%$ at one label per class vs.\ $36\%$ for $p=2$. Code: https://github.com/orenlivne/np.

</details>


### [133] [Mixture-of-Gaussians-Guided Schedule Design for Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03517)
*Ron Levi,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models (BBDM)缺乏原理性调度设计框架的问题，基于混合高斯先验提出了一套完整的调度优化准则，分别针对感知质量(Wasserstein准则)和重建保真度(MSE准则)，揭示了二者间的固有权衡，并在理论、合成数据和真实图像任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BBDM的调度设计依赖启发式方法，缺乏原理性的分析框架来指导调度选择。

Method: 基于混合高斯(MoG)先验对BBDM反向动力学进行解析分析，得到闭合形式的理想后验和MMSE去噪器，并构建可解析的替代模型来刻画BBDM的重建规律，进而提出Wasserstein（感知质量）和MSE（重建保真度）两种互补的调度设计准则，并证明存在与退化和先验无关的通用调度。

Result: 在受控MoG设置上理论与实践完全一致；在FFHQ数据集上的inpainting、deblurring和super-resolution任务验证了所提调度准则的实际价值。

Conclusion: BBDM的调度设计存在感知质量与重建保真度之间的固有权衡，且存在通用的、不依赖具体退化和先验的调度方案。

Abstract: Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models (BBDM) offer an appealing framework for image restoration and inverse problems by constructing a stochastic bridge from the clean signal directly to the degraded observation, rather than to pure noise. Despite their promise, the choice of bridge schedule is typically inherited from heuristics, and a principled analytical framework for schedule design has been lacking. In this work, we develop such a framework by offering a novel analysis of BBDM reverse dynamics under a Mixture-of-Gaussians (MoG) prior. This setting yields a closed-form ideal posterior and a corresponding MMSE denoiser, while the BBDM-induced reconstruction law is captured analytically through a tractable surrogate. Building on these expressions, we formulate two complementary schedule-design objectives: a Wasserstein criterion targeting perceptual quality and an MSE criterion targeting reconstruction fidelity. Our work exposes an inherent tradeoff between the two and proves the existence of universal schedules for both that are independent of the degradation and prior.
